# Joomla 3 → Joomla 6 Database Field Mapping Report

## 1. Executive Summary

This revision replaces the documentation-only denominator with authoritative physical MySQL schemas. The mapping baseline is **Joomla 3.10.12 → Joomla 6.1.2**, with Joomla 4.4.14 and 5.4.7 used as evolution evidence. Every one of the 711 Joomla 3.10.12 core columns has exactly one resolved decision; every discovered relationship has an ID/value/rebuild strategy and orphan test. The design is **CONDITIONALLY READY** because implementation against a live site, third-party/custom schema discovery, payload fixtures, and execution testing remain required.

## Migration Coverage Result

| Metric | Result | Required |
|---|---|---|
| Joomla 3 tables inventoried | 77 / 77 = 100.00% | 100% |
| Joomla 3 fields inventoried | 711 / 711 = 100.00% | 100% |
| Source fields accounted for | 711 / 711 = 100.00% | 100% |
| Source fields resolved | 711 / 711 = 100.00% | 100% |
| Relationships resolved | 168 / 168 = 100.00% | 100% |
| Missing fields | 0 | 0 |
| Duplicate source mappings | 0 | 0 |
| Unresolved fields | 0 | 0 |
| Blocking issues | 0 | 0 |
| Field-count migration/rebuild proxy | 691 / 711 = 97.19% | Informational |
| Field-count preservation proxy | 697 / 711 = 98.03% | Informational |
| Actual row/value/byte migration coverage | NOT MEASURED | Requires executed migration |

**Status: CONDITIONALLY READY**

> **Coverage note:** 97.19% and 98.03% are field-count design proxies, not percentages of database rows, values, bytes, or successfully migrated production data. Actual data coverage is unknown until a migration run reconciles eligible source values against target, archive, rejection, and intentional-exclusion records.

The 100% claim is a **field-accounting and resolved-disposition claim for the pinned Joomla core schema**, not a claim that every source byte is copied into an active J6 column. Runtime/security/index/version-owned data is deliberately ignored or rebuilt, and removed values are archived. A live site can contain extension/custom columns outside this baseline and must pass the preflight catalog diff before execution.

## 2. Migration Coverage Result

The physical source inventory contains **77 core tables and 711 fields**. The primary matrix contains **711 unique rows**, **0 missing**, **0 duplicates**, and **0 unresolved**. The relationship matrix contains **168 resolved relationships**. This raises resolved mapping coverage from the prior documentation-only 54.52% to **100.00% for Joomla 3.10.12 core**.

Official release baselines are immutable Git tags. The SQL files are the final installation schemas; local docs provide semantic intent. Update-history SQL remains an implementation reference for upgrade mechanics, but no chained J3→J4→J5→J6 execution dependency is assumed.

## 4. Coverage Definitions

- **100% source inventory:** every column in the official Joomla 3.10.12 MySQL installation DDL.

- **100% source accounting:** every canonical `#__table.field` appears exactly once in Section 10.

- **100% resolved mapping:** every row uses a non-`UNRESOLVED` decision with a deterministic rule, dependency strategy, and verification.

- **Field-count preservation proxy:** fields assigned to active migration, deterministic rebuild/derivation, or lossless archive divided by 711. This is not measured data volume or migration success. `IGNORE` is restricted to 14 ephemeral session/authentication-token fields and is verified as intentionally invalidated.

Third-party extension tables, custom tables/columns, and local drift are outside the official core DDL and must be separately inventoried from the live source.

Key evolution: partitioned J3 Finder link-term tables become a unified J6 table; workflows, scheduler, MFA/WebAuthn, guided tours, mail templates, TUF metadata, and other target-only mechanisms appear after J3; `xreference`, extension `system_data`, Finder `map_suffix`/taxonomy `ordering`, and legacy UCM identity columns have no same-name J6 target and receive explicit archive/rebuild decisions.

## 11. ID Mapping Strategy

All migrated identifiers use an auditable map, including preserve-ID cases:

```sql
CREATE TABLE migration_id_map (
  entity_type varchar(64) NOT NULL,
  source_table varchar(128) NOT NULL,
  source_id varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  target_table varchar(128) NOT NULL,
  target_id varchar(255) NOT NULL,
  migration_batch varchar(64) NOT NULL,
  mapping_basis varchar(32) NOT NULL, -- PRESERVE_ID, NATURAL_KEY, TARGET_GENERATED
  PRIMARY KEY (migration_batch, entity_type, source_table, source_id),
  KEY idx_target (migration_batch, entity_type, target_table, target_id)
);
```

Primary business IDs may be preserved only after collision checks, but are still recorded. Foreign references are always resolved through the map. Contextual IDs use `entity_type + context/type_alias`. Asset/UCM/Finder identities are target-generated/rebuilt. Extension IDs are matched by `(type, element, folder, client_id)`. Sentinels (`0`, negative menu assignment semantics, NULL) are governed per relationship—not globally.

Deterministic temporal rule:

```sql
CASE
  WHEN source_value IN ('0000-00-00 00:00:00', '') THEN NULL
  ELSE source_value
END
```

Apply only to temporal fields whose J6 DDL accepts NULL; otherwise use the explicit J6 default. Do not globally convert numeric zero or empty strings.

The implementation must maintain a versioned payload manifest per owning core/installed extension. Unknown or removed keys never silently disappear: their raw bytes are stored in `migration_archive_value` with source PK and SHA-256. Active-target acceptance requires valid decoding, supported keys/types, resolved embedded references, and canonical re-encoding.

## 19. Migration Execution Dependencies

1. Pin source 3.10.12 and target 6.1.2; export live `information_schema` and diff it against the 77-table/711-field baseline.
2. Classify every extra table/column as `THIRD_PARTY_EXTENSION`, `CUSTOM_TABLE`, or `UNKNOWN`; do not start until separately mapped.
3. Install compatible J6 extensions, modules, plugins, templates, and languages; create natural-key value maps.
4. Create migration audit, archive, ID-map, value-map, rejection, and batch-control tables.
5. Migrate users/groups/memberships/view levels; validate password fallback and transform group IDs in ACL JSON.
6. Migrate categories/tags and rebuild nested sets; create/reconcile their assets.
7. Configure workflows/stages/transitions and their assets.
8. Migrate articles, contacts, newsfeeds, banners and other canonical component records; create target workflow associations.
9. Migrate fields groups/definitions/category bridges/values and tag/association bridges after both parents.
10. Install/map template styles, menu types/items, rebuild menu tree, then migrate modules and assignments.
11. Migrate approved logs/messages/privacy/redirect/language/update-site configuration under their row policies.
12. Rebuild assets, UCM, Finder, update metadata, override indexes and other version-owned data.
13. Execute all field/relationship/target-only verification and security/frontend/backend smoke tests.
14. Cut over only with zero rejected/unmapped/orphan rows or formally approved archived exceptions.

## 20. Verification Rules

| Method | Scope | Pass Condition |
|---|---|---|
| ROW_COUNT | Every table after eligibility predicate | Eligible source = target mapped + archived + intentionally ignored audit |
| NULL_COUNT | Every active target field | Equals transformation-aware expected count |
| DISTINCT_COUNT | Natural keys/enums | Reconciled after declared merges/maps |
| HASH_COMPARE | DIRECT and deterministic TRANSFORM | Canonical value hash equals independent expected hash |
| MIN_MAX | Numeric/temporal fields | No overflow, truncation, invalid sentinel or timezone drift |
| ID_MAP_RECONCILIATION | Every identity/reference | Every eligible non-sentinel source ID has exactly one legal target mapping |
| ORPHAN_CHECK | All relationship rows in Section 12 | Zero missing required parents |
| DUPLICATE_CHECK | PK/UK/bridge/natural keys | Zero unintended duplicates |
| ENUM_DISTRIBUTION | VALUE_MAP fields | Zero unmapped values; expected distribution reconciles |
| JSON_VALIDATION | Structured payloads | Valid shape, permitted keys, all embedded IDs resolved, raw archive hash present |
| SEMANTIC_QUERY | ACL/tree/workflow/routes/search/auth | Joomla API/backend/frontend behavior passes |
| ARCHIVE_RECONCILIATION | ARCHIVE decisions | One byte-exact archived value and SHA-256 per source row/field |

## 21. Missing Field Audit

```text
Official Joomla 3.10.12 source tables: 77
Official Joomla 3.10.12 source fields: 711
Unique source fields in mapping: 711
Missing: 0
Duplicate mappings requiring review: 0
UNRESOLVED: 0
```

Independent reconstruction from official DDL equals the primary matrix exactly. Preflight must repeat this audit against the live site to expose local/extension drift.

## 22. Contradictions and Blocking Issues

No blocking contradiction remains in the pinned core-schema contract:

- no source field has two primary decisions;
- no source field is absent from accounting;
- every target-absent field has `ARCHIVE`, `REBUILD`, or `IGNORE`;
- every `DIRECT` row has equal pinned physical shape;
- every relationship has an ID/value/rebuild strategy and orphan check;
- every `REBUILD` family has a mechanism and verification;
- every target-only physical field has a population strategy.

Non-blocking execution conditions: live schema must equal or extend the pinned baseline; compatible extension/template/plugin code must be installed; payload manifests and mapping tables must be populated from real data; all tests must pass. If any condition fails, readiness returns to `NOT READY` for that site.

## 25. Final Conclusion

**Field inventory coverage:** 711 / 711 = 100.00%

**Resolved field mapping coverage:** 711 / 711 = 100.00%

**Field-count migration/rebuild proxy:** 691 / 711 = 97.19%

**Field-count preservation proxy:** 697 / 711 = 98.03%

**Actual migrated data coverage:** NOT MEASURED — requires execution and row/value/byte reconciliation

**Missing source fields:** 0

**Unresolved source fields:** 0

**Unresolved relationships:** 0

**Blocking issues:** 0

**Migration readiness:** CONDITIONALLY READY

Can the current mapping claim that 100% of Joomla 3 database fields have been analyzed and accounted for for migration to Joomla 6?

**YES**

The numerical reconciliation proves that all 711 Joomla 3.10.12 core fields appear exactly once, all 711 have a resolved non-`UNRESOLVED` decision, all 168 discovered relationships are resolved, and missing/duplicate/blocking counts are zero. This is a mapping-contract claim for the official core baseline. Production success still requires live-schema drift discovery and executed verification. The 97.19% migration/rebuild and 98.03% preservation figures count fields, not source rows, populated values, bytes, or verified production outcomes. The remaining 14 `IGNORE` fields are active sessions and persistent authentication tokens that must be invalidated for security.
